Routing exactly as L3546–3564, with these settlements (Deviation 17): the error exits of L3551 apply to `critic` and `revise` too (A2 T2); a structurally invalid `revise` output after the repair publishes `contested` from the last validated draft instead of `fail(failed_invalid_output)` (L4551, R5 "never withheld"; A2 T3), while a structurally invalid `generate` after the repair fails as L3553 says.
  - `load_context`: `SELECT … FROM score_targets WHERE id = :t AND claim_id = :c AND attempt_no = :a AND state = 'in_progress' FOR SHARE` (zero rows → `outcome = "claim_lost"`, END, nothing written); the attempt row (its pinned `execution_config_id` and `evaluation_policy_id`; A2 T5, Deviation 17: a reclaim creates a new attempt that pins the **active** configuration at its claim, L3491, and only an in-process resume keeps the pinned one); an `llm_results` row for the 5-tuple → `outcome = "already_published"` and the publish links it (L3523, defensive); otherwise the resume: the newest `score_drafts` row of an earlier attempt of this target whose attempt has the same `evaluation_policy_id` and tuple, or the target's `resume_draft_id` (a batch follow-up), sets `current_draft_id`, `round` and `critic_rounds` (the count of `critic_notes` on that attempt's drafts) and `resumed = True`; `start_stage = "enlarged"` when `last_call_of_attempt` of the previous attempt (or the target's `state_reason = 'batch_max_tokens'`, A2 T17) ended with `max_tokens` at a `max_tokens` ≥ the pinned first stage, else `"first"` (M2 Carry-forward J2). `repair_used` starts `False` on every attempt (A2 T6: the repair is per attempt).
  - `generate`: `logical_call_no = attempt.logical_calls + 1`; `pinned_node_config("generate", …)`; `score_prompt("generate", …)`; `gateway.call(node, prompt, CallContext(owner=TargetAttemptOwner(...), purpose_class="scoring", execution_config_id, deadline=monotonic() + node.deadline_s, attempt_id, target_id, round=0, logical_call_no, start_stage, bulk_operation_id=<the target's, only when the attempt is a funded follow-up of a batched target: Task 16 sets it on the attempt>))`; `parse_generator(result.parsed)`; a `StructuralFailure` → `repair_used` routing (L3552–3553); a draft → one transaction: `afault_point("score.generate.before_draft")`, the owner lock, `INSERT score_drafts (attempt_id, round = 0, llm_call_id, output_json = <internal draft>, validation = '{}', deterministic_failures = '{}', provisional = '{}', triggers = '{}')` (the NOT NULL columns take empty placeholders until `validate` fills them; A2 T1, Deviation 17), `afault_point("score.generate.after_draft")`.
  - `validate`: loads the draft, the revision (`description_text`, the structured fields), the profile, the det facts and the **active** scope (`active_config FOR SHARE`, L4400); `validate_draft`; `derive_knockouts` + `required_facts`; the provisional `aggregate` under the active scope with `confirmations` from the last critic note (or `unconfirmed`/`structured`); `triggers.evaluate(... sample_key=f"{target_id}:{llm_tuple_id}")`; writes `validation` (issues, repairs, groundings as spans and field names: no quotes repeated), `deterministic_failures`, `provisional` (`L`, `U`, `M`, `coverage`, `confidence`, `caps_applied`, `knockouts`) and `triggers` on the draft; sets `triggers` and `det_failures` in state.
  - `critic`: `critic_rounds + 1 ≤ 3`; effort `critic_effort(triggers, policy, normal=<policy critic effort>, light=<critic_light_effort>)`; the prompt with `<draft>`, `<required_facts>`, `<validator_issues>`; `llm_calls.round` = the critic round (1–3; A2 T8); `parse_critic`; `validate_review`; one transaction: `INSERT critic_notes (attempt_id, round, draft_id, llm_call_id, effort, triggers, verdict_returned, derived_outcome, review_json, validation)`; `critic_rounds += 1`, `last_critic_note_id`; `jobhunter_critic_verdict_mismatch_total` incremented after the commit (L4429); routing per L3557–3561 with `det_failures` recomputed from the draft's failures minus the review's `fact_rejected` facts (a rejected fact is dropped, L4555).
  - `revise`: `round + 1 ≤ 2`; `<previous_draft>` and `<issues>` (the validator's failures and issues, the critic's grounded disputes with suggested levels and quotes, `fact_rejected` / `fact_uncertain` and unreviewed required facts, the injection assessment; L4544); the generator schema; `score_drafts(round + 1)`; → `validate`.
  - `llm_step.outcome_of(exc: BaseException) -> StepOutcome(error_code, attempt_outcome, target_state, reason, backoff: bool)`, the table of A2 §2.6: `Refused(category)` → `refused` / `refused(category or 'unspecified')`; `MaxTokensExhausted` → `failed_max_tokens`; `InvalidOutput('structural')` after the repair → `failed_invalid_output`; `InvalidOutput('unexpected_stop_reason')` → `failed_invalid_output('unexpected_stop_reason')`; `LLMRequestError(400|401|403|404|413|422)` → `failed_request(str(status))`; `ModelMismatch` → `failed_request('model_mismatch')`; `LLMTransient`, `DeadlineExhausted`, a `TimeoutPolicy` cancellation → `transient_error` with backoff (`pending(not_before)` while `charged_attempts < max_score_attempts`, else `failed_transient`); `BudgetExhausted` → `budget_exhausted` / `budget_deferred('budget_exhausted')`; `ClaimLost`/`FencedOut` → `claim_lost` (the target untouched); `InputUnbounded` → `failed_request('input_unbounded')` and `LLMNotConfigured` → `transient_error` without charging (A2 T11, Deviation 17).

### Task 10: The `score_target` subgraph, part 2: the publish transaction, the contested null-out, `fail`, the claim heartbeat, and crash cases C8, C10–C13, C16 and C28 in process

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/src/jobhunter/graphs/score/nodes/{publish,fail}.py`, `graphs/score/claims.py` (`ClaimHeartbeats`), `backend/src/jobhunter/scoring/publish.py` *(new)* (the transaction, shared with the batch decision `published` of Task 17 and the eval runs of Task 19), `scoring/contested.py` *(new)* (the null-out of §11.10)
- Test: `tests/unit/test_contested.py`, `tests/integration/test_publish.py`, `tests/integration/test_score_target_fail.py`, `tests/integration/test_score_target_crashes.py`, `tests/integration/test_claim_heartbeat.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9's state and nodes; `PublishedResult`, `repair_for_publication`, `aggregate`, `det_results.write_instance`, `det_inputs` (Tasks 5–6); `transition` (`score_targets`, `score_attempts`); `ranking.bump`; `raise_alert`; `heartbeat_loop(beat, *, period_s, on_lost)` (`worker/heartbeat.py:33–51`); `spend_ledger.raise_cap_reached`; `is_fenced`; `dedup.groups` (the representative update of publish step 7).
- Produces:
  - `contested.null_out(draft: GeneratorDraft, validation: DraftValidation, last_review: CriticValidation | None) -> Contested(draft, nulled_criteria: list[str], dropped_facts: list[str], not_a_job_ad: bool, badge_possibly_not_a_job_ad: bool)`, L4551–4558 verbatim: criteria with an unresolved V2/V3 failure or V4 consistency failure → `level = null, basis = unknown`, listed in `nulled_criteria`; a fact with failed grounding is treated as not asserted (its knockout dropped); a `fact_rejected` fact dropped, `uncertain` and `unconfirmed` kept as badges without a cap; `not_a_job_ad` kept only when `not_a_job_ad:ad` was confirmed with a grounded quote, otherwise published as a real ad with the badge; semantic disputes keep the validated level with the suggested level displayed (kept in `critic_notes`, not the result).
  - `publish.publish_result(conn, *, target: TargetRow, attempt_id: int, final_draft_id: int, mode: Literal["accepted", "contested"], predicate: ClaimPredicate | BatchPredicate, now) -> PublishOutcome(result_id | None, det_result_ids: list[int], reason: Literal["published", "claim_lost", "fenced"])`, the transaction of L3531–3541 in order: 1. `active_config FOR SHARE` (and its `scope_version_id`, `pending_scope_version_id`); 1a. the job `FOR SHARE` and `is_fenced(job_id, llm_tuple_id)` → `fenced`: nothing published, the attempt ends `cancelled('purge_fenced')` and the target is left for the purge's chunk (L3533; A2 S4: `stuck_targets` ignores an `in_progress` target of a fenced job, Task 18); 2. the target `FOR UPDATE` with the claim predicate (or `state = 'in_batch' AND batch_submission_id = :s AND attempt_no = :a`) → zero rows `claim_lost`, the draft stays; 3. the publication object: `contested.null_out` when `mode = "contested"`, then `repair_for_publication`, then `PublishedResult.model_validate` — a failure is a `Bug`: the attempt ends `failed_invalid_output('publication_invalid')`, the target `failed_invalid_output('publication_invalid')`, and the critical alert `scoring.publication_invalid` (subject the target id; A2 S6, Deviation 18); 4. `INSERT llm_results` (`criteria`, `job_facts`, `knockout_candidates`, `fact_confirmations`, `not_a_job_ad`, `injection_suspected`, `nulled_criteria`, `truncated_fields`, `critic_outcome` ∈ `not_run | accepted | contested`, `revisions_used`, the two summaries, the five version ids) and `result_evidence` rows from the groundings of the final draft and the last review (`subject` = criterion key or fact id, `source_kind`, `field_name`, `quote` 4–300, description spans); 5. `det_results.compute_and_write` for the active scope and, when `pending_scope_version_id` is set, for the pending scope; 6. `transition("score_targets", id, [from], published_*, owner=predicate, set_={"published_result_id": r})` (the claim or batch columns cleared by `clear_on_exit`); 7. the duplicate group: `groups.representative_published(conn, job_id)` keeps deferred members deferred (the `all_failed`/`all_filtered` re-election is Task 15's); `transition("score_attempts", attempt_id, ["running"], published_*)`; `ranking.bump` last. The deferred FKs check at commit (L3541). Fault points `score.publish.before_commit` and `score.publish.after_commit`.
  - `nodes.publish`: calls `publish_result` with `mode` from state and records the `TargetOutcome`. `nodes.fail`: applies `llm_step.outcome_of`'s result in one transaction: the target transition from `in_progress` with the claim predicate (`budget_deferred('budget_exhausted')`, `refused(category)` with `refusal_category`, `failed_*` with `state_reason`, `pending` with `not_before = now + min(6 h, 15 min × 2^(transient_failures))` and `transient_failures + 1` for a transient error with `charged_attempts < 4`, `failed_transient` otherwise; the claim columns cleared), the attempt `running → <outcome>` with `outcome_detail`, and for `budget_exhausted` `spend_ledger.raise_cap_reached(conn, month, quarantined_usd=<Σ reserved_usd of in-flight batch items>, now)` after the owner row and under the month lock (A2 T15; the lock order owner → month holds). A `claim_lost` outcome writes nothing to the target; the attempt row is ended by whoever changed the predicate (a reclaim writes `claim_lost`, a cancellation `cancelled`; A2 T12, Deviation 17) and `fail` only logs `score_claim_lost`.
  - `claims.ClaimHeartbeats(engine, *, period_s=30, monotonic)`: `start(ref: ClaimRef, task: asyncio.Task)` runs `heartbeat_loop(beat, period_s=30, on_lost=task.cancel)` with `beat` = `UPDATE score_targets SET claim_heartbeat_at = now() WHERE id = :t AND claim_id = :c AND state = 'in_progress'` (L3572; 0 rows → lost); `stop(ref)`; `stop_all()`. The subgraph's `load_context` registers the current task; `publish` and `fail` stop it; the cycle task's finally stops all. A cancelled coroutine's in-flight stream settles `ambiguous` through the gateway's `CancelledError` path (M2 V11).

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/src/jobhunter/graphs/score/graph.py`, `graphs/score/nodes/{admit,claim,finalize_cycle}.py`, `backend/src/jobhunter/scoring/cycles.py`
- Modify: `backend/src/jobhunter/worker/tasks.py` (`score_cycle`), `worker/tick.py` (step 6 in its own transaction), `worker/executions.py` (`ExecKey` gains `"cycle"`), `worker/runtime.py` (`score_graphs`, `score_deps`), `worker/main.py` (the runtime build; registration lands in Task 27), `backend/src/jobhunter/graphs/outcomes.py` (`merge_outcomes` generic), `privacy/retention.py` (the `cycle-` thread prefix is Task 23's; `THREAD_PREFIXES` gains it here so retention finds it)
- Test: `tests/unit/test_score_graph_shape.py`, `tests/integration/test_cycles.py`, `tests/integration/test_atomic_claim.py`, `tests/integration/test_score_graph.py`, `tests/integration/test_orphaned_cycles.py`, `tests/integration/test_c29_cycle_crash.py`, `tests/crash/test_c29_score_cycle.py` (the real-worker form runs in Task 28)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9/10's subgraph and `ScoreContext`; `admission.admit` (Task 12, consumed through a stub `admit_all_score_now` until then; the full allocation lands in Task 12); `transition_where`, `transition`; `fence_epoch`, `register_takeover_sweep`; `run_owned(key, token, work, *, beat, period_s, registry)`, `ExecutionRegistry`; `heartbeat_loop`; `defer_in_tx`; `run_tick`'s `TickReport`; `lock_month`, `read_month`, `budget.bucket_capacity`, `central_cost_per_target`; `is_fenced`; `ShutdownState.admission_stopped`; `open_alert`, `resolve_alert`.
- Produces:
  - `cycles.c_hat(conn, *, llm_tuple_id, evaluation_policy_id, model_policy: ModelPolicy, now) -> tuple[Decimal, Literal["history", "central"]]`: the trailing 7-day mean of `score_attempts.cost_usd` over **online attempts with `charged = true` that ended `published_accepted` or `published_contested`** under the tuple and policy, when at least 20 exist; otherwise `central_cost_per_target(model_policy)` (L1174; A2 A3, Deviation 20).
  - `cycles.pause_reason(conn) -> str | None`: `"reservation_bound_violated"` while an **unacknowledged** open `llm.reservation_bound_violated` exists (`acknowledged_at IS NULL`), `"auth_failed"` while `llm.auth_failed` is open, `"cap_reached"` while `llm.cap_reached` is open, `"global_fence"` while a global purge fence is active, `"schema_unverified"` while the active prompt-schema version has no `schema_dry_run` eval run with `passed = true` and the worker is not in fake-LLM mode (M2 Carry-forward 1, Deviation 5); `None` otherwise. Scoring pauses at the cycle trigger (M2 Deviation 11; A2 T16, Deviation 19): no cycle starts while a reason holds, and the health section and `/api/system/spend` report it.
  - `async cycles.start_cycle(conn, *, trigger: CycleTrigger, now, epoch: int, defer: Deferrer) -> int | None`: `fence_epoch` first; `active_config FOR SHARE` (the tuple, scope and evaluation policy; `None` with no tuple); `pause_reason` → `None`; `periodic` and `manual` need pending targets of the tuple with `not_before` passed (A2 A7) and `bucket_usd ≥ min(min_cycle_targets × ĉ, B_max)` under the month-row lock (L1176); `score_now` needs a pending `lane = 'score_now'` target of the tuple; `batch_followup` needs a pending `lane = 'batch_followup'` target and is bucket-gated like `periodic` (A2 G6, Deviation 20); `S = max(0, min(max_targets_per_cycle, floor(bucket_usd / ĉ)))` for `periodic`, `manual` and `batch_followup`, `0` for `score_now` (A2 G5/G7); the insert `INSERT INTO score_cycles (trigger, llm_tuple_id, scope_version_id, evaluation_policy_id, worker_epoch, status, bucket_usd_at_start, c_hat_usd, admission_size)` — a `score_cycles_one_running` violation (`UniqueViolation`) returns `None` (the running cycle serves the trigger through its next claim round, L3499); then `defer(conn, "score_cycle", lock="score", queueing_lock=f"cycle:{id}", cycle_id=id)`.
  - `worker/tick.py`: `run_tick` is unchanged; `tick_score(engine, *, now, epoch, defer) -> TickScoreReport(started: int | None, pause_reason: str | None)` runs `start_cycle(trigger="periodic")` in its own transaction after `run_tick` (§3.4 step 6), and the tick task calls both.
  - `graph.build_score_graph(deps, subgraph) -> CompiledGraph` on the worker's saver: `START → admit → claim → (Send("score_target", ref) for each claim | finalize_cycle when none) → more → (claim | finalize_cycle) → END`, where `more` is a conditional edge that returns to `claim` while pending `lane IN ('score_now', 'batch_followup')` targets of the cycle's tuple exist and `claim_rounds < MAX_CLAIM_ROUNDS = 5` (the "next claim round" of L3499; A2 G3/G4, Deviation 20; the trigger is recorded by the target's lane and the deferred job that then finds a running cycle). `CycleState` verbatim (L3450–3457) plus `claim_rounds: int` and `claimed` with an append reducer. Invoke: `ainvoke(input, config={"configurable": {"thread_id": f"cycle-{cycle_id}", "cycle": ctx}, "recursion_limit": 200, "max_concurrency": 3}, durability="sync")` (L3374, L3469).
  - `nodes.admit`: `active_config FOR SHARE`; the cycle owner predicate (`score_cycles … WHERE id = :c AND worker_epoch = :e AND status = 'running' FOR SHARE`); the sweeps and the allocation (`admission.admit`, Task 12); writes `admitted` (counts per lane) and returns `(target_id, lane)` pairs in admission order; bumps `ranking_state` when a sweep moved a target (A2 G13, Deviation 20).
  - `nodes.claim`: `fence_epoch`; `active_config FOR SHARE` for the **active** `execution_config_id` and `evaluation_policy_id` (L3491); the cycle predicate; the atomic claim as L3477–3488 through `transition_where("score_targets", ["pending"], "in_progress", where="t.id = a.id AND t.id IN (SELECT id FROM score_targets WHERE id = ANY(:ids) AND state = 'pending' AND (not_before IS NULL OR not_before <= now()) AND NOT is_fenced(job_id, llm_tuple_id) FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED)", params={"ids": ids, "lanes": lanes, "epoch": epoch}, set_sql={"claim_id": "gen_random_uuid()", "claim_epoch": ":epoch", "claimed_at": "now()", "claim_heartbeat_at": "now()", "attempt_no": "t.attempt_no + 1", "lane": "a.lane"}, returning=("id", "claim_id", "attempt_no"))` with the `FROM unnest(:ids, :lanes) AS a(id, lane)` clause; one `score_attempts(target_id, attempt_no, mode = 'online', outcome = 'running', claim_id, claim_epoch, cycle_id, execution_config_id, evaluation_policy_id)` row per returned target; `ranking.bump` last; the `ClaimRef`s into state and `ClaimHeartbeats.start` for each.
  - `nodes.finalize_cycle`: the report `{"outcomes": {outcome: n}, "lanes": {lane: n}, "spent_usd": Σ score_attempts.cost_usd of the cycle, "cache": <CacheMonitor snapshot: reads, writes, hit ratio>, "c_hat": …, "admission_size": S, "claim_rounds": n}` (ids and counts only); `transition("score_cycles", id, ["running"], "completed", owner={"worker_epoch": epoch}, set_={"report": …, "spent_usd": …})`; resolves `score_cycle.orphaned`; evaluates `scoring.critic_rate_high` (Task 18's function); never defers another cycle (L3470).
  - `tasks.score_cycle(context, cycle_id)` on queue `score`, `lock="score"`, `queueing_lock=f"cycle:{cycle_id}"` (the stalled-job rule of `release_stalled_jobs` binds it: a stalled `score_cycle` ends `failed` and the orphan reconciler fails its cycle; M2 Carry-forward I4): `run_owned(("cycle", cycle_id), token=epoch, work=<the graph invocation>, beat=<UPDATE score_cycles SET heartbeat_at = now() WHERE id = :c AND worker_epoch = :e AND status = 'running'>, period_s=30)`; a lost beat cancels the graph (its claims are reclaimed by the reconciler); an unclassified exception fails the cycle `failed('bug')` with the critical alert `score_cycle.failed` (A2 S5, Deviation 18) and reclaims its claims.
  - Takeover sweeps (`register_takeover_sweep`): `"score_targets"` (§3.3.1 step 3, L262): `in_progress` targets with `claim_epoch < :epoch` → `pending` (charged attempts left) or `failed_attempts_exhausted`, their attempts `claim_lost`, through `transition_where`; `"score_cycles"` (step 4, L263): `running` cycles with `worker_epoch < :epoch` → `failed('orphaned')` with the report closed `{"reason": "orphaned"}`. The `orphaned_cycles` tick reconciler does the same for `heartbeat_at < now() − 3 min` and raises `score_cycle.orphaned` (warn, L5596), resolved by the next completed cycle.

  - `admission.LANES: Final = (("similarity", Decimal("0.70")), ("oldest", Decimal("0.20")), ("exploration", Decimal("0.10")))`, `SHARE`, `TIE_ORDER = {"oldest": 0, "exploration": 1, "similarity": 2}` (L3605–3607), `MAX_SCORE_ATTEMPTS: Final = 4` (L2863, `max_score_attempts`), `ALWAYS_INCLUDE_LIMIT(S, slots) = min(slots["similarity"], S // 2)` (the property test L5697 "never exceeds half of `S`" governs L3635's `ceil(0.5 * S)`: Deviation 24).
  - Pure: `allocate(credits: Mapping[Lane, Fraction], S: int) -> Allocation(slots: dict[Lane, int], credits: dict[Lane, Fraction])`, L3617–3629 verbatim with exact fractions: accrual capped at `share × S + 1`, whole credits to slots, the trim loop (largest excess over `share × S`, `TIE_ORDER` on ties), largest-remainder leftovers, `assert sum(slots.values()) == S`; `select(sets: CandidateSets, slots, S, *, score_now: Sequence[int]) -> Selection(picked: list[Picked(target_id, lane, always_include_reason | None)], taken: dict[Lane, int], spilled: dict[Lane, int])`, L3614–3651 verbatim: `score_now` first (unbounded, outside `S`), always-include from the similarity slots up to `ALWAYS_INCLUDE_LIMIT`, similarity for the remainder, oldest and exploration by slot, then the spill by actual remaining capacity in the order oldest, exploration, similarity; credits are debited only for slot-backed takes (`credit -= taken[lane]`), spills cost nothing; "nothing is ever rejected; the rest stays pending".
  - `CandidateSets(similarity: list[int], oldest: list[int], exploration: list[int], always_include: list[tuple[int, AlwaysIncludeReason]])`, each list in lane order, built by `candidates(conn, *, llm_tuple_id, cycle_id, scope_version_id, profile_version_id, now) -> CandidateSets` from `pending` targets of the tuple with `not_before` passed and `NOT is_fenced(job_id, llm_tuple_id)` (L3654): `similarity` ordered by `similarity DESC` with `similarity IS NOT NULL`, **excluding** targets whose revision is `partial` and non-representative members of an active group (L3655); `oldest` by `created_at ASC`; `exploration` by `md5(:cycle_id || ':' || id)` (a reproducible random order seeded by the cycle id, L3657); `always_include` (L3631–3633), highest similarity first and NULL last: full-text twins (`completeness = 'full'` members of a group whose representative is `partial`, §8.7 re-election), watchlist companies (jobs with a posting from a `source_instances` row of a watchlist company), title trigram `similarity(jobs.title_norm, <target title>) >= 0.6` against any confirmed target title of the profile (`profile_versions.profile_json -> 'target_titles'`, normalised with `normalize.titles.normalize_title`), and batch follow-ups (`lane = 'batch_followup'`), with `always_include_reason` ∈ `watchlist | title_match | full_text_twin | batch_followup` (L2221–2222). Null-similarity targets are eligible for oldest, exploration and always-include (L3662). The queries use `score_targets_admission` and `score_targets_oldest`.
  - `LaneCredits`: `async lock(conn, llm_tuple_id) -> LaneCredits` (`admission_lane_credits … FOR UPDATE`, three rows inserted at 0 when absent), `.credits: dict[Lane, Fraction]`, `async save(conn)` (stored as `numeric(10,4)`, rounded half-even; the CHECK `credit >= -1` holds because a remainder slot borrows at most one, L3658).
  - `async admission.admit(conn, cycle: CycleRow, *, now, sweeps: Sweeps) -> list[Picked]`: `sweeps.expiry(conn, …)`, `sweeps.refilter(conn, …)`, `sweeps.regroup(conn, …)` (Task 13's functions, L3610–3612), then `candidates`, `LaneCredits.lock`, `allocate(credits, S)`, `select`, `credits.save()`, `UPDATE score_cycles SET admitted = :counts WHERE id = :c AND worker_epoch = :e` (the cycle owner), and the pairs in admission order. The lane and the always-include reason are recorded on the target by the claim (`lane = a.lane`, `always_include_reason = a.reason`; A2 A2 and A1 issue 20, Deviation 24: the reason survives the claim's lane overwrite of a batch follow-up, and the bulk accounting identifies batched targets through `batch_items`, never through `lane`).

  - `async targets.regroup_sweep(conn, *, llm_tuple_id) -> Swept(deferred: int, undeferred: int)`: `pending | budget_deferred` members that satisfy the §8.7 deferral predicate (L3261) → `deferred_duplicate` with `deferred_group_id`; `deferred_duplicate` members that no longer do (the group dissolved, the member is the representative, `score_individually`, a conflicting fact) → `pending`; overrides untouched.
  - `admission.Sweeps` is the triple above; `admit` runs them in that order (L3610–3612) under the cycle's tuple and scope and bumps `ranking_state` when any moved a target.
  - `@reconciler("expiry_sweep", "heavy")`: the expiry sweep for the active tuple with the active `max_ad_age_days` (L5513 "every 10 min + every cycle"); `@reconciler("budget_release", "tick")` (L1201, L5506): `fence_epoch`; `active_config FOR SHARE`; the month row `FOR UPDATE` (`lock_month`); `k = floor(headroom("scoring") / r_first(active policy, execution))`; when `k ≥ 1`, the `k` oldest `budget_deferred` targets of the active tuple (`ORDER BY state_changed_at`) → `pending` through `transition_where` (`CLEAR_REASON`), `resolve_cap_reached` when the alert is open, and `ranking.bump`.
  - `async targets.score_now(conn, *, job_id: int, force: bool, now, epoch, defer) -> ScoreNow(target_id, state) | ScoreNowRefused(code)` (§9.6.4 L3675–3678): the job's current-revision target of the active tuple (`active_config FOR SHARE`; `not_found` without one); the accepted states: `pending | budget_deferred | expired_unscored | deferred_duplicate` → `pending` with `override = 'score_now'`, `override_at = now`, `lane = 'score_now'`, the group and filter columns cleared (L2879); `refused | failed_*` take the Retry edge (`charged_attempts = 0`, L2880); `filtered_out` only with `force = true` (`override = 'score_anyway'`, `filter_scope_version_id` cleared, L2876), else `filtered_requires_force`; `in_progress | in_batch | published_*` → `conflict_state`; the 409 pre-check: `read_month`'s scoring headroom `< r_first` → `budget_exhausted` and nothing changes (L3678; A2 T18, Deviation 24: a headroom check against `r_first`, racy by design, the attempt's own reservation is the authority); then `cycles.start_cycle(trigger="score_now")` (a running cycle serves it in its next claim round); `ranking.bump`.

The routes of Task 21 call these three.
  - `alerts.acknowledge_alert(conn, alert_id: int, *, now) -> bool`: sets `acknowledged_at` on an open alert (the score-lane pause of `llm.reservation_bound_violated` ends with it, Task 11's `pause_reason`; the route is Task 21's).
  - `save._apply_admission`: when `scope.max_ad_age_days` changed, `expiry_sweep` for the active tuple in the save transaction (L4893 "an immediate expiry sweep in both directions"; A2 A5, Deviation 24: one statement pair under the lock the save already holds); `_apply_budget` is unchanged (a raised cap returns deferred targets through `budget_release` at the next tick, L4894).

A tuple that is unchanged (the same profile and policy, L4786) opens no bulk operation and creates no operation (every target already exists); the confirm's `targets_to_create` stays the estimate's count (M2 Deviation 42).
  - The `create_targets_for_tuple` handler (`register_operation_handler("create_targets_for_tuple")`), payload `{"llm_tuple_id", "bulk_operation_id"}`: chunks of 500 current revisions of retained, unmerged, untombstoned jobs (L4795), ordered `availability = 'active'` first, then jobs with `user_job_actions.saved_at` or `applied_at` set, then the rest by `jobs.id`; each chunk one transaction: `active_config FOR SHARE` (the tuple still active, else the operation ends `succeeded` as obsolete with `progress = {"skipped": "tuple_superseded"}`), the owner row, `create_or_revive` per job (the job rows `FOR SHARE`), `set_progress({"done", "total"})`, `ranking.bump`; fault point `create_targets.after_chunk`; when every chunk is done and `settings.scoring.batch_bulk_rescore` holds and at least `batch_min_targets` targets of the operation are `pending` with `override = 'none'`, `create_operation(kind="batch_submit", payload={"bulk_operation_id": b, "llm_tuple_id": t}, dedup_key=f"batch_submit:{b}:1", defer=...)` deferred with `lock="batch"` (`defer_operation_job(conn, defer, op_id, lock="batch")`; L284, A2 R4: the settable `batch_min_targets` governs L3708's "≥ 200"); then `finish_in(succeeded)`.
  - Node 9 (`enqueue_targets`, `SCORE_TARGETS_ENABLED = True`; L3431, R9, Deviation 9): with `state["llm_tuple_id"]` set, one transaction per chunk of 500 revisions created or changed in this run (`job_revisions.created_at >= runs.started_at` for the run's jobs, the node-8 pattern): `active_config FOR SHARE`; when the run's tuple is no longer the active one the node creates nothing and records `{"targets": {"skipped": "tuple_superseded"}}` (the confirm's `create_targets_for_tuple` owns the new tuple); otherwise `create_or_revive` for each current revision without a target of the run's tuple, and `cancel_targets(revision_ids=<revisions that stopped being current>, reason="revision_superseded")` for the run's tuple (A2 R2); the counts go to `state["counters"]`. Timeout 5 min, `DB_RETRY`.
  - I5 (`finalize._i5`): evaluated against **the run's tuple** (`runs.llm_tuple_id`): every current revision created or changed in this run has a target for it, or the run's tuple is NULL; when the run's tuple is no longer active at finalize, I5 passes with `detail = {"llm_tuple_id": t, "targets": "tuple_superseded"}` (R9). The report's `revisions.targets` becomes `{"created": n, "revived": n, "cancelled": n, "skipped": {...}}` and `build_report` moves to `graphs/collect/report.py`.
  - `@reconciler("missing_targets", "heavy")` (L3718, L5509, §7.17 #2): for the active tuple, current revisions of retained unmerged jobs without a target, in chunks of 500, `create_or_revive` with the job `FOR SHARE` and the fence (C36: a purge chunk holds the job `FOR UPDATE`, so the reconciler waits and then sees the fence or the deleted job), `ranking.bump` per chunk.

- Produces:
  - `batches.BatchesClient(cfg, *, http_client: httpx2.AsyncClient, clock)`: the raw SDK `AsyncAnthropic(base_url="https://api.anthropic.com", api_key=<the anthropic_key secret>, max_retries=0, timeout=anthropic.Timeout(connect=10, read=120, write=60, pool=10), http_client=http_client)` (L3937; the shared client keeps the fake mount, and the per-request `timeout` carries the batches' `write=60`), inside `llm/` only (the boundary lint). Methods: `async create(requests: Sequence[BatchRequest]) -> Created(provider_batch_id, created_at, expires_at, request_counts) | Rejected(status: int, error_type: str, message: str) | Ambiguous(error_class: str)` (a definitive 4xx is `Rejected`; a timeout, connection loss, 5xx or 529 is `Ambiguous`; the call runs under `asyncio.timeout(CREATE_DEADLINE_S = 600)`, the "10-minute client deadline" of L3985; A2 B11), `async retrieve(id) -> BatchStatus(processing_status, request_counts, ended_at, expires_at)`, `async results(id) -> AsyncIterator[ItemResult(custom_id, result_type, message | error | None)]` plus `ResultsStream.complete: bool` after the iterator ends without a transport error, `async cancel(id) -> BatchStatus`, `async list_all() -> AsyncIterator[BatchSummary(id, created_at, request_counts, processing_status)]` (auto-paginating `limit=100`, `after_id`). `BatchRequest(custom_id: str, params: Mapping[str, JSONValue])`. Every method logs ids, counts and codes only.
  - `spend_ledger.reserve_batch(conn, *, owner: BatchSubmissionOwner, items: Sequence[BatchItemReservation], model, effort, max_tokens, execution_config_id, prompt_template_version, bulk_operation_id, now) -> list[Reservation]`: one `current_params`, the owner lock, one month lock, the `bulk_operations` row `FOR UPDATE`, then per item the worst case at batch prices, the admission check against the hard cap (every item, L1159 "including batches"), the ledger insert (`transport = 'batch'`, `batch = true`, `bulk_operation_id`, `logical_call_no` NULL, `admitted_buffer_usd`) and the `llm_calls` insert (`transport = 'batch'`, `attempt_id`, `target_id`, `batch_item_id`, `round = 0`), `spent_usd += Σ r` on the bulk row; a `BudgetExhausted` on any item aborts the whole prepare (nothing is written). `BatchItemReservation(target_id, attempt_id, batch_item_id, input_tokens_bound, rendered_prompt_sha256, prefix_sha256, input_refs)`.
  - `bulk.hold_per_item(conn, *, bulk_operation_id, policy: EvaluationPolicy, model_policy) -> Decimal` = `projected_critic_rate × c_hat_followup(model_policy)` (L1190); `bulk.add_hold(conn, bulk_operation_id, usd)`, `bulk.release_hold(conn, bulk_operation_id, usd)` (never below 0), both under the row `FOR UPDATE`; `bulk.fits(conn, *, bulk_operation_id, worst_case_usd: Decimal, hold_usd: Decimal) -> bool` = `spent_usd + followup_held_usd + worst_case_usd + hold_usd ≤ budget_usd` (L1189, §7.17 #7).
  - `scoring/batches.py`:
    - `ELIGIBLE_SQL`: the §10.10 checklist of L3939 (A2 §5.4) as one static predicate over `score_targets t`: `t.bulk_operation_id = :b AND t.state = 'pending' AND (t.not_before IS NULL OR t.not_before <= now()) AND NOT is_fenced(t.job_id, t.llm_tuple_id) AND t.resume_draft_id IS NULL AND (t.lane IS NULL OR t.lane NOT IN ('batch_followup', 'score_now')) AND (t.state_reason IS NULL OR t.state_reason NOT IN ('batch_max_tokens', 'batch_invalid_output', 'batch_errored', 'batch_canceled', 'batch_expired', 'batch_missing')) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM batch_items i WHERE i.target_id = t.id AND i.result_type <> 'awaiting') AND t.charged_attempts < 4 AND t.override = 'none'`; the order `availability = 'active' DESC, <previous tuple's score_mid> DESC NULLS LAST, t.similarity DESC NULLS LAST, t.id` (L3942; the previous tuple's `score_mid` through the job's newest other-tuple current instance).
    - `async prepare(conn, *, bulk_operation_id, llm_tuple_id, settings: Settings, now) -> Prepared(submission_id, request_count, reserved_usd, hold_usd) | NotPrepared(reason: Literal["batches_disabled", "in_flight", "ambiguous", "below_minimum", "no_allocation"])`, the Prepare transaction of L3951 under the month row: `active_config FOR SHARE` (the active execution config and evaluation policy for the attempts, L3813), the preconditions (`batch_bulk_rescore`, no in-flight or `submit_ambiguous` submission: the `batch_one_in_flight` index is the last word), the candidates by `ELIGIBLE_SQL` cut to `batch_max_items` and to `BATCH_MAX_BYTES = 200 * 1024 * 1024` of rendered params (the provider's 256 MB, A2 B14; Deviation 27), then to the largest `n` whose cumulative worst case plus `n × hold` fits (`bulk.fits` per prefix, each item's worst case from its own rendered bound), `n < batch_min_targets` → `NotPrepared("below_minimum")`; `INSERT batch_submissions (state = 'preparing', llm_tuple_id, bulk_operation_id, request_count = n, request_hash = sha256(ordered (custom_id ‖ sha256(canonical params))), reserved_usd = Σ, followup_held_usd = n × hold)`; per target the CAS `pending → in_batch` through `transition_where(where=ELIGIBLE_SQL + " AND t.id = ANY(:ids)", set_sql={"attempt_no": "t.attempt_no + 1", "lane": "'batch'", "batch_submission_id": ":s"})`; `INSERT score_attempts (mode = 'batch', outcome = 'running', execution_config_id, evaluation_policy_id)` per returned target; `INSERT batch_items (submission_id, target_id, attempt_no, custom_id = 't<target>_a<attempt>')`; `reserve_batch` with `BatchSubmissionOwner(submission_id, "preparing")`; `batch_items.llm_call_id` set (the deferred pair, L2572–2573); `bulk.add_hold`. The rendered params per item: `batch_request_body(node, prompt, max_tokens=<first stage>)` with the prefix cache marker kept (L6622). A target whose CAS returned nothing (claimed meanwhile) is dropped from the submission before the insert of its rows (the count and hash are computed over the returned set).
    - `async submit_start(conn, submission_id, *, now)`: `preparing → submit_started` (the `submit_started_at` changed-at) and `mark_batch_dispatched` (R6; `charged_attempts + 1` on every target, the attempts `charged`); fault points `batch.before_submit_start` and `batch.after_submit_start`.
    - `async record_created(conn, submission_id, created: Created, *, now)`: `submit_started → submitted` with `provider_batch_id`, `expires_at = created.created_at + 24 h` (L3954); `async record_rejected(conn, submission_id, rejected: Rejected, *, now)`: `submit_started → submit_rejected`; every item entry `settle(outcome="not_billed", evidence="batch_submit_rejected", call=CallOutcome(status="error", http_status=rejected.status, error_code="batch_submit_rejected"))`; the targets `in_batch → pending` with `batch_submission_id` cleared (owner `batch_submission_id = :s AND attempt_no = :a`); the attempts `submit_rejected`; `bulk.release_hold(n × hold)`; the follow-on (below). `async record_ambiguous(conn, submission_id, ambiguous: Ambiguous, *, now)`: `submit_started → submit_ambiguous` with `reconcile_report = {"error_class": …}`; nothing else moves (L3956).
    - `async abandon(conn, submission_id, *, now)` (step 4d): `preparing → abandoned`; `release_never_dispatched` per item entry (the calls `cancelled`); the targets → `pending`; the attempts `cancelled` with `outcome_detail = 'batch_abandoned'` (A2 B10, Deviation 12); the hold released; the follow-on. `register_takeover_sweep("batch_submissions")`: every `preparing` submission whose `batch_submit` operation is not `running` is abandoned at takeover; the `batch_submit` handler abandons a stale `preparing` submission of its operation before preparing anew.
    - `async follow_on(conn, *, bulk_operation_id, llm_tuple_id, defer)`: when a submission ends `reconciled`, `released`, `submit_rejected` or `abandoned` (L3945; "ends" means those four states, not `ended`: A2 B13), and the bulk operation is still `active`, `create_operation(kind="batch_submit", payload={...}, dedup_key=f"batch_submit:{bulk}:{seq + 1}", defer=...)` deferred with `lock="batch"`, where `seq` is the number of the operation's submissions; a released submission triggers it at the release only.
    - The `batch_submit` handler (`register_operation_handler("batch_submit")`), payload `{"bulk_operation_id", "llm_tuple_id"}`: in order, `abandon` of a stale `preparing` row; the three sweeps for the tuple (L3939 "the same sweeps as online admission"); `prepare` (a `NotPrepared` ends the operation `succeeded` with `progress = {"skipped": reason}`); `submit_start` in its own transaction; `BatchesClient.create` outside any transaction; `record_created | record_rejected | record_ambiguous` in its own transaction; `finish_in(succeeded)` (the submission's state is the outcome). The global purge fence refuses the submit before `submit_start` (L6365).
  - `tests/payload/test_batch_params.py` (L3932): the batch params of an item equal the online generator body for the same prompt except `stream`, with `cache_control` on `system[1]` and `max_tokens` = the first stage.

### Task 17: The Message Batches branch, part 2: polling, item decisions, missing items, cancellation, ambiguous-submit reconciliation, release, and crash cases C20–C23 and C33

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/src/jobhunter/scoring/batches.py` (`poll`, `process_item`, `reconcile_ambiguous`, `release`, `cancel_requested`), `backend/src/jobhunter/worker/tasks.py` (the periodic tasks `batch_poll` `*/5 * * * *` and `batch_reconcile_ambiguous` `*/15 * * * *` on `aux`, each with a `queueing_lock` of its name; A2 B16, Deviation 27: periodic tasks, since the reconciler framework has no 5- or 15-minute cadence), `scoring/cycles.py` (the `batch_followup` trigger), `graphs/score/nodes/{publish,fail}.py` (the follow-up hold release when a resumed attempt ends), `api/routes/spend.py` (`quarantined_batch_usd`, Task 21 finishes the route)
- Test: `tests/integration/test_batch_poll.py`, `tests/integration/test_batch_decisions.py`, `tests/integration/test_batch_ambiguous.py`, `tests/integration/test_batch_release.py`, `tests/integration/test_batch_crashes.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `BatchesClient`, the Task 16 lifecycle, `BatchScript` outcomes (Task 8); `settle` with the batch evidences and `ambiguous` (Task 7); `parse_generator`, `validate_step` (Tasks 5, 9), `publish_result` with the batch predicate (Task 10); `transition`, `transition_where`; `cycles.start_cycle(trigger="batch_followup")`; `raise_alert`, `open_alert`, `resolve_alert`; `bulk.release_hold`; `targets.cancel_targets`.
- Produces:
  - `async batches.poll(engine, client, *, now, epoch, defer) -> PollReport`, the body of the `batch_poll` task (L3958): for each `submitted` submission: a pending cancel (`cancel_requested_at IS NOT NULL` and the batch not yet ended) → `client.cancel` (3 tries, then logged, L6443), then `retrieve`; `processing_status = 'ended'` → `submitted → ended` (`ended_at`); for each `ended` submission: stream `results()`, `process_item` per line in its own transaction, and when the stream was read to its end (`complete`) and every line's `custom_id` belongs to the submission: `results_complete_at`, then every unprocessed item → `missing` with its entry `dispatched → ambiguous` (A1 issue 22) and the decision `retry_online` → `pending('batch_missing')`, `batch.items_missing` (critical, subject the submission id); then `ended → reconciled` (`reconciled_at`), `bulk.complete_if_done`, `follow_on`; a cut stream leaves the submission `ended` for the next poll (C23's idempotency on `processed_at`); at least one `followup` decision → `cycles.start_cycle(trigger="batch_followup")`. A `submitted` submission past `expires_at + 1 h` without `ended` is retrieved like any other (the provider ends it with expired items).
  - `async batches.process_item(conn, *, submission, item, result: ItemResult, now) -> Decision`: idempotent on `batch_items.processed_at` (a second call returns the stored decision); the owner `BatchSubmissionOwner(submission.id, submission.state)`; **spend first**: `succeeded` → `settle(outcome="billed", usage=<from the message>, call=CallOutcome(status="completed", model_served, stop_reason, output_json=<the wire JSON>, provider_request_id=<message id>))` at batch prices (the ledger row's `batch` flag); `errored | canceled | expired` → `not_billed(batch_item_errored | batch_item_canceled | batch_item_expired)` with the call `error`; a `released` or already-`ambiguous` entry takes the late edges `ambiguous → billed | not_billed` (L2974–2975, Codex N9). **Then the decision**: the target `FOR UPDATE` with `state = 'in_batch' AND batch_submission_id = :s AND attempt_no = :a` (L3959) → otherwise `ignored_fenced` (the attempt, if still `running`, ends `cancelled('ignored_fenced')`; A2 B1: a cancel requested for a superseded tuple finds its targets already cancelled, so `canceled` items of that submission are `ignored_fenced`, Deviation 27). With the fence passed, the table of L3962–3976 verbatim: `succeeded`/`end_turn` → `parse_generator` → a `StructuralFailure` → `retry_online` → `pending('batch_invalid_output')` with no draft and the attempt `failed_invalid_output('batch_invalid_output')`; valid → `score_drafts(round = 0)` under the batch attempt, `validate_step` (the §9.4 `validate` logic: V1–V6, knockouts, the provisional aggregation under the active scope, T1–T8 under the **batch attempt's** policy, L3967; A2 R6: the follow-up attempt re-validates under its own policy at claim, so a policy change between them may publish without a critic, as §11.11 allows) → no trigger and no failure → `publish_result(mode="accepted", predicate=<batch>)` (`published`, the attempt `published_accepted`) else `followup`: `in_batch → pending` with `lane = 'batch_followup'`, `resume_draft_id`, `batch_submission_id` cleared, the attempt `batch_draft_ready`; `stop_reason = refusal` → `refused(category)` with `refusal_category`, the attempt `refused`; `max_tokens` → `retry_online` → `pending('batch_max_tokens')` (the attempt `failed_max_tokens`; Task 9's `load_context` starts the next attempt enlarged); any other stop → `failed_invalid_output('unexpected_stop_reason')`; `errored`/`invalid_request` → `failed_request('batch_invalid_request')` (the attempt `failed_request`); `errored`/other → `retry_online` → `pending('batch_errored')` (the attempt `transient_error`); `canceled` with `cancel_requested_at` set → the fence (above), otherwise `retry_online` → `pending('batch_canceled')`; `expired` → `retry_online` → `pending('batch_expired')`. Every `retry_online` target is never batched again (`ELIGIBLE_SQL`, Task 16). Each decision writes `batch_items (result_type, stop_reason, decision, processed_at)` and, for every decision but `followup`, `bulk.release_hold(hold)` (A2 B3/B4, Deviation 27: a `followup` keeps its hold until the resumed attempt ends, which `publish`/`fail` release through `score_attempts.resumed_from_attempt_id`; the `bulk_operations` reconciler releases whatever is left at completion).
  - `async batches.reconcile_ambiguous(engine, client, *, now, epoch) -> ReconcileReport`, the body of `batch_reconcile_ambiguous` (L3984–3999), for each `submit_ambiguous` submission and each `released` one with `submit_started_at > now − 29 days` (A2 B12): `client.list_all()`; a listing error leaves every row untouched and logged (`batch_listing_failed`); the window `created_at ∈ [submit_started_at − 1 min, submit_started_at + 10 min]`; candidates = the window's batches whose `request_counts` total equals `request_count`; for each candidate that has `ended`: download its results completely; the `custom_id` set equal to ours → **confirmed**: `provider_batch_id` adopted, `submit_ambiguous | released → ended` (`ended_at`), the items processed per `process_item` (for a released submission every item is `ignored_fenced` and only its spend settles, L3999), then `ended → reconciled` and, for a `submit_ambiguous` one, the follow-on; a partial intersection → `batch.custom_id_anomaly` (critical, subject the submission id) and the submission stays; otherwise `last_reconcile_at = now` and `reconcile_report = {"candidates": n, "listed": m}`; a `submit_ambiguous` submission with `submit_started_at < now − 25 h` → `open_alert("batch.submit_ambiguous_unresolved", critical, subject, context={"reserved_usd": …})` (never a transition, chair decision C3); a `released` submission past 29 days is listed no more. The workspace is assumed to be this installation's alone (A2 B15, Deviation 27).
  - `async batches.release(conn, submission_id, *, now, defer) -> Released(reserved_usd: Decimal)` (L3991–3997): `submit_ambiguous → released` (`released_at`); the targets `in_batch → pending` (owner `batch_submission_id = :s AND attempt_no = :a`); the attempts `released`; every item entry `dispatched → ambiguous` (`settle(outcome="ambiguous", call=CallOutcome(status="ambiguous"))`), counted at its reservation; the holds released; `batch.submit_ambiguous_unresolved` resolved; the follow-on. The route is Task 21's (`POST /api/batches/{id}/release`).
  - `batches.quarantined_usd(conn) -> Decimal`: Σ `reserved_usd` of `submit_ambiguous` submissions (`llm.cap_reached`'s context, `/api/system/spend`).

The takeover sweep of Task 11 shares the statements.
  - `@reconciler("det_completeness", "heavy")` (L5511, §7.17 #5): every published result of the active tuple without a current instance for the active scope (and the pending scope while one exists) whose hash and version are current → `compute_and_write`, in chunks of 500 under `active_config FOR SHARE`; a changed `DET_ALGORITHM_VERSION` therefore recomputes every current instance (L4568).
  - `@reconciler("admission_alerts", "heavy")` (§9.6.5, L3686–3688; A2 A8, Deviation 28): `admission.exploration_hits` (warn): over the trailing 14 days, with ≥ 10 published targets whose attempt's `lane = 'exploration'`, more than 25% with the current instance's `score_mid ≥ relevance_threshold`; resolved below the threshold; `admission.high_sim_expired` (warn): ≥ 3 targets that entered `expired_unscored` in the last 24 h with `similarity ≥` the 70th percentile of `similarity` over the targets admitted in the similarity lane in the trailing 7 days (`score_attempts.started_at` joined to `score_targets.lane = 'similarity'`); resolved after 24 h without recurrence; `admission.backlog_drain` (info): `count(pending of the active tuple) / (admitted in the trailing 7 days / 7) > max_ad_age_days`, raised at most once a day (subject the UTC date).
  - `@reconciler("scoring_drift", "heavy")` (§11.13, L4633–4635): `scoring.feedback_disagreement` (warn): over 14 days with ≥ 10 `score_feedback` rows, more than 30% are `poor` on snapshots with `M ≥ τ` or `good` with `M < τ − 20`; `scoring.distribution_shift` (info): the 7-day mean `score_mid` of the active tuple's current instances differs by more than 10 from the previous 28 days' mean under the same tuple; `scoring.critic_rate_high` (warn, L5595): `critic_rate_7d` = first-round critic calls ÷ attempts with ≥ 1 dispatched call over the trailing 7 days, n ≥ 50 attempts, `> 1.5 × projected_critic_rate` of the active policy; evaluated here and at every `finalize_cycle`.
  - `cycles.critic_rate_7d(conn, *, llm_tuple_id, evaluation_policy_id, now) -> tuple[Decimal | None, int]` (the rate and `n`), also used by `/api/system/spend` (Task 21).
  - `budget_counters` (M2, heavy) also recomputes each active bulk operation's `spent_usd` from the ledger (reserved, dispatched and ambiguous entries at `reserved_usd`, billed at `settled_usd`, not_billed at 0, for entries with that `bulk_operation_id`) and corrects it under the row lock with `llm.budget_counter_drift` (§7.17 #7's first clause; A1 issue 29, Deviation 28).
  - Metrics (§17.5 L5538–5539): `jobhunter_critic_rate_7d` (gauge), `jobhunter_critic_verdict_mismatch_total` (counter, Task 9 increments it), `jobhunter_score_targets{state}` (gauge per state), `jobhunter_oldest_pending_target_age_seconds` (gauge), `jobhunter_claim_reclaims_total` (counter); `metrics_refresh` (M1, heavy) sets the three gauges.
  - `health.system_health` gains `scoring: {running_cycle_id, pause_reason, oldest_pending_target_age_s, pending_targets, in_batch_targets, quarantined_batch_usd}` (L5552 "oldest pending target age"), read without locks.
  - `llm.auth_failed`'s pause ends with an acknowledgement as well as with a billed try (`pause_reason` reads `acknowledged_at`; B R11, Deviation 19): a paused lane makes no scoring try, and the non-scoring calls (a profile extraction, a dry run) resolve it when they succeed.

Synthetic jobs get no posting, no target (`create_targets_for_tuple`, node 9 and `missing_targets` select `NOT synthetic_eval`), no embedding and never appear in `current_scores` for a real tuple (their revisions have no target). `jobhunter golden seed-adversarial [--fixtures <dir>]` runs it (default `tests/eval/fixtures/adversarial`, packaged with the wheel under `jobhunter.scoring.eval_fixtures` for the dev stack).
  - `eval_runner.run_eval(engine, gateway, *, eval_run_id: int, now) -> EvalOutcome`, the `eval_run` operation handler's body (`register_operation_handler("eval_run")`, payload `{"eval_run_id"}`, dedup key `eval_run:<id>`): reads the run's candidate (`rubric_version_id`, `prompt_schema_version_id`, `model_policy_id`, `evaluation_policy_id`, `execution_config_id`, `route`, `repetitions`); for each active case (every case for `kind = 'golden'`, the `is_canary` cases for `kind = 'canary'`) and repetition: the job `FOR SHARE` and `is_fenced(job_id)` → skipped and reported `fenced`, no row (L6362); the case's hard filters under the case scope from the frozen snapshot (`det_facts_from_inputs`) → `eval_case_results(outcome = 'filtered_out', critic_ran = false, llm_call_ids = [], output = {"filters": …})`; otherwise the production route with the steps of Task 9 over an in-memory `DraftStore`, the candidate's rubric and prompt-schema version rendered as for a target, `CallContext(owner=EvalRunOwner(eval_run_id), purpose_class="nonscoring", execution_config_id, eval_run_id, golden_case_id, round, logical_call_no, deadline)` (every call carries `golden_case_id`, L4611; the ledger books it non-scoring under the reserve, L2341), the triggers under the candidate policy with the case's T8 key, `route = 'forced_critic'` forcing one critic round (L4612), the contested null-out and aggregation under the case scope from the snapshot; `eval_case_results (outcome ∈ accepted | contested | refused | failed, critic_ran, llm_call_ids, output = <the publication object plus the rounds' triggers and derived outcomes>, score_mid)`; a gateway failure of a case is `failed` and the run goes on. The run ends `completed` with `metrics` from Task 20's `calibration.metrics` and `passed` from `calibration.gate`, `cost_usd` = Σ settled (ambiguous at reserved); an abandoned run is failed by `llm_ledger_unsettled`'s `EVAL_RUN_ABANDONED` rule (M2). `jobhunter eval run --kind golden --candidate active|<rubric>,<prompt_schema>,<model_policy>,<evaluation_policy>,<execution_config> [--route forced_critic] [--repetitions 2] (--live | --fake)` inserts the `eval_runs` row and the operation and waits for it (the manual workflow of Task 20 runs it live).

### Task 20: The eval gate, the calibration metrics, canaries, `projected_critic_rate` replay and `eval-manual.yml`

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/src/jobhunter/scoring/calibration.py`, `.github/workflows/eval-manual.yml`, `tests/unit/test_calibration.py`, `tests/integration/test_eval_gate.py`, `tests/integration/test_canaries.py`, `tests/integration/test_projected_critic_rate.py`
- Modify: `backend/src/jobhunter/settings/validate.py` (`eval_gate_state(conn, *, candidate=None)`), `settings/dryrun.py`, `settings/save.py` (`_check_eval_gate` with the candidate), `settings/derive.py`, `db/seed.py` (`ensure_evaluation_policy` with the replay), `scoring/evaluation_policy.py` (`replay_projected_critic_rate`), `worker/tasks.py` (`canary_daily` `41 6 * * *`), `tests/unit/test_settings_validate.py`, `tests/integration/test_settings_save.py`, `tests/integration/test_settings_dry_run.py` (the gate now reads runs)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `eval_runs`, `eval_case_results`, `golden_cases` (Task 1); `run_eval`, `golden.tuple_identity` (Task 19); `GateState`, `eval_gate_state`, `warnings_only`, `_check_eval_gate`, `_GATED` (`settings/validate.py:59–323`, `settings/save.py:192–204`); `ensure_evaluation_policy` (`db/seed.py:211–227`), `DEFAULT_PROJECTED_CRITIC_RATE`; `score_drafts.provisional`, `.validation`, `.triggers`; `raise_alert`.
- Produces:
  - `calibration.Metrics` (frozen dataclass) and `calibration.metrics(results: Sequence[CaseResult], *, cases: Mapping[int, CaseRow], projected_critic_rate: Decimal, central_cost_usd: Decimal, similarity_cutoff: Decimal | None) -> Metrics`, pure, with `CaseResult(golden_case_id, repetition, outcome, critic_ran, score_mid, nulled_criteria, grounding_ok: bool, schema_ok: bool, knockouts_applied: list[str], hard_filtered: bool, cost_usd)`; judged with `ordinal(good) = 2, ok = 1, poor = 0` on the production route (L4614): `schema_validity` = the share of case results that are `accepted | contested | refused | filtered_out` (a `failed` outcome is a schema or transport failure); `grounding_rate` = the share of published accepted ratings whose every evidence quote grounded; `spearman_rho` over `(score_mid, ordinal)` of published cases with average ranks for ties (A3 G20, Deviation 29); `pairwise_agreement` = the share of case pairs with different labels whose `score_mid` order matches the label order; `false_exclusions` = `good` cases that were hard-filtered or carry a binding hard knockout; `knockout_recall` = the share of `expected_knockouts` that appear in the case's applied binding knockouts (cases without expectations are excluded; A3 G21: vacuous for week-1 cases); `contested_rate` over published cases; `test_retest_sd` = the mean over the ten lowest-id active cases with two repetitions of the SD of their `score_mid` (`None` when `repetitions < 2`); `per_profile` for every profile with ≥ 10 cases (`rho`, `contested_rate`, `below_pooled: bool`); reported only: `critic_rate` vs `projected_critic_rate`, `admission_recall` (the share of `good` cases whose target similarity reaches `similarity_cutoff`, `None` without one), `mean_cost_usd` vs `central_cost_usd`.
  - `calibration.gate(m: Metrics, *, active_cases: int) -> GateState`: `"uncalibrated"` while `active_cases < 30` (L4631); `"passed"` iff `schema_validity == 1`, `grounding_rate == 1`, `spearman_rho ≥ 0.6`, `pairwise_agreement ≥ 0.75`, `false_exclusions == 0`, `knockout_recall == 1`, `contested_rate ≤ 0.15` and (`test_retest_sd is None or ≤ 5`) (L4616–4625); else `"failed"`.
  - `validate.Candidate(rubric_version_id: int, prompt_schema_version_id: int, model_policy_hash: bytes, evaluation_policy_hash: bytes)` and `async validate.eval_gate_state(conn, *, candidate: Candidate | None = None) -> GateState` (B R14, Deviation 29): `"uncalibrated"` while fewer than 30 active golden cases exist; otherwise the newest `completed` `eval_runs` row with `kind = 'golden'`, `route = 'production'` and the candidate's four identities (the policy rows found by hash; the execution config is not part of the identity, L4631) decides: `passed` → `"passed"`, `False` → `"failed"`, no such run → `"failed"` (a run is required). `dryrun.dry_run` and `save._check_eval_gate` build the candidate from the proposed payload (its model policy and evaluation blocks hashed by `content_hash`, the active rubric and prompt-schema pointers); the shipped-version activations of Task 21 pass theirs. `eval.gate_failed` (warn, L5618) is raised when a golden run of a candidate ends `passed = false`. `_check_eval_gate` also refuses an `llm_tuple` save whose generator or reviser model has no passed **live** `schema_dry_run` run for the active prompt-schema version (`metrics.cases` names the model) with 409 `conflict_state` (`detail = "schema_dry_run_required"`), because compilation is proven per model (A3 G30, Deviation 5); the dry-run CLI takes `--model-policy <id>` to run the cases of a candidate policy.
  - `evaluation_policy.replay_projected_critic_rate(conn, policy: EvaluationPolicy, *, now) -> Decimal | None` (§11.7.4 L4480): over the round-0 `score_drafts` of the last 30 days (≥ 50 rows, else `None`), the share whose stored `provisional` and `validation` would trigger any of T1–T8 under the new thresholds (`triggers.evaluate` re-run with `critic_rounds = 0` on the stored figures); `ensure_evaluation_policy` stores it at the row's creation and `DEFAULT_PROJECTED_CRITIC_RATE` otherwise (M2 Deviation 16 closed). The rate is fixed at creation: an identical payload reuses its row and its rate (B R9, Deviation 29; L4480 "computed when a policy row is created").
  - `canary_daily` (periodic `41 6 * * *` on the `tick` queue, `queueing_lock = "canary_daily"`, L285): with a tuple active and at least one `is_canary` active case, `INSERT eval_runs (kind = 'canary', route = 'production', <the active rubric, prompt schema, model policy, evaluation policy and execution config>)` and `create_operation(kind="eval_run", payload={"eval_run_id"}, dedup_key=f"eval_run:{id}")` (the runner of Task 19 scores the canaries under their own profile and scope, L4632); `calibration.canary_drift(conn, *, eval_run_id) -> list[Drift]` after the run: for each canary, `|score_mid − baseline| > 8` against the **first** canary `score_mid` under the key `(golden_case_id, rubric_version_id, prompt_schema_version_id, model_policy_id, evaluation_policy_id)` (A1 issue 25, Deviation 29) → `scoring.canary_drift` (warn, subject the case id).
  - `.github/workflows/eval-manual.yml` (§18.6 L5818–5828): `workflow_dispatch` with inputs `candidate` (`active` or the five ids), `route`, `repetitions`; the dry-run workflow's throwaway PostgreSQL and secret files; `jobhunter migrate`, `jobhunter golden seed-adversarial`, then `jobhunter eval run --live …` with the repository secret `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY_EVAL`; the metrics table printed and a markdown report uploaded as a workflow artifact; the secret files removed `if: always()`. It never runs on push or pull request.

  - `POST /api/settings/rubric-versions/{id}/activate` and `POST /api/settings/prompt-schema-versions/{id}/activate` `{expected_rescore_ack: bool, eval_override_ack?: bool}` → 201 `{version}` (L5005–5006; §13.3 L4900): `activate.activate_shipped_version(conn, *, kind, version_id, req, now, defer) -> SaveOutcome` under `active_config FOR UPDATE` then `run_idempotent`: an unknown id → 404; the prompt-schema form requires a `schema_dry_run` eval run **for that version** with `passed = true` and `metrics ->> 'transport' = 'live'` (M2 Carry-forward 1; B R13: queried by version, never "the latest run") → else 409 `conflict_state` with `detail = "schema_dry_run_required"`; the eval gate for the candidate (the new pointer with the active others) → 409 `eval_gate_failed` without `eval_override_ack`; `expected_rescore_ack` required while retained revisions exist (as the confirm, M2 Deviation 42) → else 422; then the pointer flip (`UPDATE active_config SET rubric_version_id | prompt_schema_version_id = :id`), and an internal `save_settings` of the active payload with the effect class `llm_tuple` forced (`SaveRequest(..., eval_override_ack)`), which runs `ensure_tuple` on the new pointers and `targets.activate_tuple` (Task 14) and records the override on the new settings version (Deviation 23); the 201 body `{version: <version_no>, llm_tuple_id, targets_to_create, bulk_budget_usd}`.
  - `GET /api/settings/rubric-versions` → `[{id, version, content_hash, created_at, active}]` and `GET /api/settings/prompt-schema-versions` → `[{id, prompt_schema_hash, created_at, active, dry_run: {eval_run_id, passed, transport, finished_at} | null}]` (the newest dry-run of that version), so the id to activate is discoverable (Deviation 23; M4's "new rubric v2 available" reads them).
  - `POST /api/jobs/{job_id}/score-now` `{force?: bool}` → 202 `{target_id, state}`; 409 `budget_exhausted`, 409 `filtered_requires_force`, 409 `conflict_state`, 404 (L4976; `active_config FOR SHARE` then `run_idempotent` then `targets.score_now`). `POST /api/targets/{target_id}/retry` → 202 `{target_id, state}` (L4985). `POST /api/duplicate-groups/{group_id}/score-individually` stays M4's (M1 Carry-forward 3; the domain function exists).
  - `POST /api/system/score-cycle` → 202 `{cycle_id: int | null, started: bool, reason: str | null}` (L5028; `cycles.start_cycle(trigger="manual")`; `reason` is the pause reason or `"running"`, `"no_pending"`, `"bucket"`).
  - `POST /api/batches/{id}/release` `{confirm: true}` → 202 `{submission_id, state: "released", reserved_usd}` (L5027); 409 `conflict_state` unless the submission is `submit_ambiguous`; 422 without `confirm: true`.
  - `POST /api/jobs/{job_id}/feedback` `{kind: "label" | "wrong_score", label: "good" | "ok" | "poor", comment?: str (≤ 2,000)}` → 201 `{feedback_id, golden_case_id}` (L4977); 409 `conflict_state` without a published result. `GET /api/golden-cases?cursor&limit&active` → keyset `[{id, job_id, label, comment, profile_version, scope_version, source, active, is_canary}]` (L4978). `DELETE /api/golden-cases/{id}` → 204 (L4979; the manifest is M5's). `PATCH /api/golden-cases/{id}` `{active?: bool, is_canary?: bool}` → 200 the row (R2).
  - `POST /api/alerts/{id}/ack` → 200 `{id, acknowledged_at}` (L5030; R10: the score-lane pause of `llm.reservation_bound_violated` and `llm.auth_failed` is lifted by it); 404 for a resolved or unknown alert.
  - `GET /api/system/spend`: `c_hat` from `cycles.c_hat` (history or central, plus `c_hat_source`), `critic_rate_7d` from `cycles.critic_rate_7d` (`null` below 50 attempts), `bulk_operation: {id, state, budget_usd, spent_usd, followup_held_usd, llm_tuple_id} | null`, `quarantined_batch_usd` from `batches.quarantined_usd`, and `pause_reason`. `SpendOut.bulk_operation` becomes `BulkOperationOut | None` (the OpenAPI document changes; `scripts/check-openapi-drift.sh` and the regenerated `schema.d.ts` land in the same commit, B R18).

```python
    body = {"force": False}
    r1, r2 = await asyncio.gather(
        logged_in_client.post(f"/api/jobs/{job_id}/score-now", json=body, headers=idem("tab-1")),
        logged_in_client.post(f"/api/jobs/{job_id}/score-now", json=body, headers=idem("tab-2")),
    )
    assert {r1.status_code, r2.status_code} == {202} and r1.json()["target_id"] == r2.json()["target_id"]
    row = await target_row(engine, pending_target)
    assert (row.override, row.lane, row.state) == ("score_now", "score_now", "pending") and row.override_at is not None
    assert await count_rows(engine, "score_cycles", trigger="score_now") == 1
    await run_cycle(engine)                                           # the running cycle claims it once
    r3 = await logged_in_client.post(f"/api/jobs/{job_id}/score-now", json=body, headers=idem("tab-3"))   # mid-cycle
    assert r3.status_code == 202 and await count_rows(engine, "score_cycles") == 1
    assert await charged_attempts(engine, pending_target) == 1
```

`tests/integration/test_api_activation.py`: activating prompt-schema v2 on an install whose pointer is v1 (the test inserts v1 as active and v2 inactive, surviving the reset) without a live dry-run is 409 `schema_dry_run_required`; with an inserted passed live run and the gate uncalibrated it answers 201, flips the pointer, creates the tuple, cancels the old tuple's targets, opens the bulk operation and records `create_targets_for_tuple`; a replay with the same key returns the stored 201; with 30 cases and no golden run it is 409 `eval_gate_failed` unless `eval_override_ack`; the rubric form the same on the rubric pointer; the listings show `active` and `dry_run`. `tests/integration/test_api_batches.py`: the release route on a `submit_ambiguous` submission (C21's user half), 409 otherwise, 422 without confirm, and the `Idempotency-Key`. `tests/integration/test_api_golden.py`: the feedback route's 201 and 409, the listing's cursor, the PATCH's canary limit, the DELETE's cascade, 422 on a NUL comment. `tests/integration/test_api_alerts.py`: the ack lifts `pause_reason` (a cycle starts afterwards), 404 on a resolved alert. `tests/integration/test_spend_api.py`: the four M3 fields with and without history. `tests/unit/test_openapi_m3.py`: every path above is in the generated document and the drift check passes.

### Task 23: Retention of the score tables, target-set deletion, and the `cycle-` checkpoint threads

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/src/jobhunter/privacy/retention.py` (`RULES`, `_DELETES`, the thread rules), `backend/src/jobhunter/scoring/targets.py` (`delete_target_set`), `tests/integration/test_retention_m3.py`; update `tests/integration/test_retention.py` (the rule order pin)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RetentionRule(name, window, depends_on)`, `RULES`, `_DELETES`, `_ENDED_THREADS`, `_PROFILE_THREADS`, `THREAD_BATCH = 500`, `_DELETE_EVAL_RUNS`, `run_retention_daily(engine, queue, saver, *, cv_store, now, epoch)` (`privacy/retention.py:148–656`); `Settings.retention.score_history_months` (§13.1 L4865, default 12; `settings/model.py:471`); `targets.cancel_targets`, `batches.request_cancel` (per-target form); `lock_active(share=True)`.
- Produces (§20.1 L6304–6305, L6308, L6312, L6320; §20.2 L6367–6378; B R13, Deviation 31):
  - `async targets.delete_target_set(conn, target_ids: Sequence[int], *, now) -> int`, the nine steps of L6369–6378 verbatim for one chunk of at most 200 targets, in one transaction: 1. the non-terminal targets of the set → `cancelled('purged')` (`in_progress` claims cleared, their attempts `cancelled`; for `in_batch` targets the submission's cancel is requested first); 2. `resume_draft_id := NULL`; 3. `result_evidence` and `det_results` of the set's results (the `score_feedback` and `golden_cases.labelled_from_result_id` references become NULL by their FKs); 4. `critic_notes` of the set's attempts; 5. `llm_results` of the set (the deferred keys with `published_result_id` are satisfied because step 9 runs in the same transaction); 6. `score_drafts` of the attempts; 7. `batch_items` of the set with their `llm_calls` (the deferred pair), then every other `llm_calls` row of the attempts (the ledger rows stay); 8. `score_attempts`; 9. the targets. M5's purges reuse it.
  - The retention rules, in `RULES` after `eval_runs` and before `checkpoints`: `score_history` (window `score_history_months` months, read from the active settings at the start of the pass): targets of **inactive** tuples that ended terminally (`published_* | refused | failed_* | expired_unscored | cancelled`) longer ago than the window (`state_changed_at`), excluding results referenced by an active golden case (`golden_cases.labelled_from_result_id` with `active`), deleted in chunks of 200 through `delete_target_set`, each chunk re-checking under `active_config FOR SHARE` that the tuple is still inactive (L6367); `det_results_superseded` (the same window): superseded instances older than the window whose result is retained; `score_cycles` and `bulk_operations` (13 months after `finished_at`, L6312); `cycle_threads` (14 days after the cycle ended, L6320): checkpoint threads named `cycle-<id>` whose cycle `finished_at < now − 14 d`, in batches of `THREAD_BATCH` (the `cycle-` prefix never matches `_PROFILE_THREADS`' `profile-%` or `queries-%`, B R17). `eval_case_results` and their calls go with `eval_runs` (M2's rule; the cascades of Task 1). Every rule keeps M2's error boundary and `retention.failed`.
  - `golden_cases` exemption (L6307): the job, its revision and its profile version of an active case are exempt from tombstoning (M1's `tombstone` rule gains `AND NOT EXISTS (active golden case)`), and a tombstoning that would orphan an inactive case deletes that case (L6307; M5's purges apply the same).

- Test: `tests/unit/test_quota_dryrun.py` (the Arbeitnow cases), `tests/unit/test_settings_effects.py`, `tests/integration/test_settings_save.py` (a timezone-only save re-anchors)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `quota/dryrun._arbeitnow(settings) -> (warnings, projection)` (`quota/dryrun.py:121–126`: `page_cap = min(10, max(3, ceil(interval_h / 30) + 1))`, `expected_pages = interval_h / ARBEITNOW_HOURS_PER_PAGE + 1`); `settings/effects.FIELD_EFFECTS` (`display.timezone` → `_NONE`, `:72`); `save._apply_schedule` (`settings/save.py:243–260`: `anchor_utc_for(a.now, schedule.anchor_local, a.proposed.display.timezone)`); `docs/spikes/V8.md` (page 1 holds 300 items and spans 02:32–20:00 on 2026-10-18, about 17.5 h; page 2 reaches back 53 h; every response is an edge-cache hit with a TTL of about one hour; the page cap stays).
- Produces (U3; M2 Deviation 39 and the M2 final review's two spec inconsistencies closed; Deviations 32 and 33):
  - `ARBEITNOW_HOURS_PER_PAGE: Final = 17.5` (V8's measured yield of page 1, the only page whose span the cap formula bounds; the old 37 came from an assumed yield) with the docstring and §6.8 L1109 changed to `expected_pages = proposed.interval_h / 17.5 + 1   # page 1 ≈ 17.5 h of postings (V8)`. The cap formula is unchanged (V8's decision). The warning now fires where it should within §13.1's 1–168 h: not at 24 h (2.4 expected pages, cap 3) or 48 h (3.7, cap 4), and from 72 h on (5.1 expected pages, cap 5; at 168 h 10.6 against a cap of 7).
  - `display.timezone` takes the effect class `schedule` (`FIELD_EFFECTS["display.timezone"] = _SCHEDULE`), and §13.1 L4820 reads `| Display | \`display.timezone\` | IANA zone; display, and the zone of \`schedule.anchor_local\` | \`Europe/Berlin\` | schedule |`. Justification (U3 asks for one): the anchor is computed from the timezone at save time (L4818), and `_apply_schedule` already re-anchors from `proposed.display.timezone`; the class makes a timezone change run that existing action and tells the user, in the dry-run's effect list, that the schedule moves. Re-anchoring on every save would re-anchor on saves that touch neither field, and would re-run the dead-man update (§17.7) for nothing.
